Duration prevents the act from evaporating, orientation gives it direction, standing gives it bodily form, and the orbit motif expresses repetition without displacement from the station.","ayah_refs":["70:22","70:33","70:34","70:36"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Fear Before Judgment and Humiliation After It","parent_semantic_invariant":"Careful fear anticipates an unsecured danger; when the danger arrives, it surrounds, lowers, and visibly masters the body.","parent_surprising_reach":"Twilight, exposed frontier, pursuing pressure, sinking star, lost hump, low hem, and struck peg all specify the transition from vigilance to abasement.","key":"B","name":"Pressure that closes over the body","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A pursuing burden catches the person, overwhelms him, lowers his profile, and leaves him under imposed disgrace.","active_motifs":"overwhelming and mastering `quranic:root_000606:B001/m01`; burdensome imposition `quranic:root_000606:B003/m01`; time narrowing toward loss `quranic:root_000606:B004/m01`; subjugated abasement `quranic:root_000519:B001/m01`; low hanging hem or structure `quranic:root_000519:B005/m01`; peg battered by blows `quranic:root_000519:B006/m01`; bodily lowering and downcast gaze `quranic:root_000412:B001/m01`; hump deprived of its height `quranic:root_000412:B004/m01`; submissive fear `quranic:root_001594:B004/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` مُهْطِعِين (`ه ط ع`); `70:44` خَاشِعَة أَبْصَارُهُم تَرْهَقُهُم ذِلَّة (`خ ش ع`, `ب ص ر`, `ر ه ق`, `ذ ل ل`).","synthesis":"Humiliation is a force with direction and load. It catches, imposes weight, drives the body down, removes the hump's proud height, and makes the person receive blows like a peg. The abstract noun becomes visible posture.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:44"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Fear Before Judgment and Humiliation After It","parent_semantic_invariant":"Careful fear anticipates an unsecured danger; when the danger arrives, it surrounds, lowers, and visibly masters the body.","parent_surprising_reach":"Twilight, exposed frontier, pursuing pressure, sinking star, lost hump, low hem, and struck peg all specify the transition from vigilance to abasement.","key":"C","name":"Eye, horizon, and sinking light","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A fixed forward gaze gives way to streaming eyes, sinking celestial bodies, and lowered sight.","active_motifs":"ocular sight `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; inward discernment `quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`; prolonged forward gaze `quranic:root_001594:B001/m01`; projected head and neck `quranic:root_001594:B002/m01`; tear channels at the eye's edges `quranic:root_001077:B004/m01`; sharp wet edges of teeth `quranic:root_001077:B005/m01`; star sinking toward setting `quranic:root_000412:B003/m01`; twilight glow `quranic:root_000803:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:44` أَبْصَار (`ب ص ر`); `70:27` مُشْفِقُون (`ش ف ق`); `70:36` مُهْطِعِين (`ه ط ع`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:44` خَاشِعَة (`خ ش ع`).","synthesis":"The crowd first projects eye, head, and neck forward; at the endpoint, sight sinks. Tear channels and setting light translate the reversal into a horizon inside the face, where the gaze reaches its own west.","ayah_refs":["70:44","70:27","70:36","70:40"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Guarded Aperture, Legitimate Pair, and Transgressive Crossing","parent_semantic_invariant":"A vulnerable opening is protected by a lawful relational boundary; seeking beyond it converts movement into trespass.","parent_surprising_reach":"Sexual ethics becomes frontier management, paired fitting, contract, hidden breach, spreading wound, and crossed riverbank.","key":"B","name":"Pairing, possession, and contractual legitimacy","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Two persons are joined as a pair through marriage, ownership, oath, and acknowledged transfer.","active_motifs":"paired counterpart `quranic:root_000652:B001/m01`; marital spouse `quranic:root_000652:B002/m01`; joining one partner to another `quranic:root_000652:B003/m01`; ownership and right-hand possession `quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`; marriage contract `quranic:root_001444:B004/m01`; right hand and right side `quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`; binding oath `quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`; right-hand power and protection `quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`; marriage payment `quranic:root_000852:B007/m01`; written guarantee `quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:26` يُصَدِّقُون (`ص د ق`); `70:30` أَزْوَاج، مَلَكَت أَيْمَان (`ز و ج`, `م ل ك`, `ي م ن`); `70:36` قِبَلَك (`ق ب ل`).","synthesis":"Legitimacy is a fitted relation with public supports: pairing supplies symmetry, contract and dower establish transfer, oath binds, guarantee answers, and the right hand combines possession with protective responsibility.","ayah_refs":["70:26","70:30","70:36"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Trust as Recurrent Care and Answerable Custody","parent_semantic_invariant":"An entrusted relation survives through repeated inspection, protective management, and liability for what has been received.","parent_surprising_reach":"Custody becomes shepherding, listening, turning a flock back, written warranty, neighborhood protection, and care of a dependent child.","key":"C","name":"Guarantee that permits recourse","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A claim is protected by a guarantor, a written warranty, and a right that must be defended if performance fails.","active_motifs":"commercial warranty `quranic:root_001055:B006/m01`; personal guarantee and written undertaking `quranic:root_001198:B008/m01`; inviolable interest requiring defense `quranic:root_000347:B007/m01`; covenantal bond `quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`; entrusted security `quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`; blood-compensation replacing retaliation `quranic:root_001119:B002/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:24` حَق (`ح ق ق`); `70:27-28` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:28,32` مَأْمُون/أَمَانَات (`ء م ن`); `70:28,30` غَيْر (`غ ي ر`); `70:32` عَهْد (`ع ه د`); `70:36` قِبَلَك (`ق ب ل`).","synthesis":"A trust becomes institutionally answerable when breach triggers recourse. Warranty, guarantor, defended interest, covenant, and compensation give custody an enforceable underside rather than leaving it to sentiment.","ayah_refs":["70:24","70:27","70:28","70:32","70:36"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Cultivated Enclosure and Honored Residence","parent_semantic_invariant":"The garden is a protected ecology whose shade, water, growth, and settled enjoyment are produced by sustained care.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garden becomes canopy, vineyard, pasture, rain-cloud, well, bucket, camel trough, catchment, fertile soil, and hospitable station.","key":"A","name":"Vegetative enclosure","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Trees and vines grow densely enough to form a sheltering interior that also supports grazing life.","active_motifs":"concealment by cover `quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`; tree-covered garden `quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`; dense, rising vegetation `quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`; fertile soil and rain `quranic:root_001294:B002/m01`; vine and grape `quranic:root_001294:B004/m01`; fruit sheath `quranic:root_001307:B010/m01`; young grape shoot `quranic:root_000698:B007/m01`; pasture `quranic:root_000574:B001/m01`; livestock wealth `quranic:root_001525:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:32` رَاعُون (`ر ع ي`); `70:35,38` جَنَّات/جَنَّة (`ج ن ن`); `70:35` مُكْرَمُون (`ك ر م`); `70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:38` نَعِيم (`ن ع م`); `70:43` سِرَاعًا (`س ر ع`).","synthesis":"A garden is created by self-thickening growth: trees hide the interior, vines bear fruit under sheaths, fertile rain renews the soil, and pasture sustains animals. Its cover is productive rather than evasive because it continuously gives outward.","ayah_refs":["70:32","70:35","70:36","70:38","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Cultivated Enclosure and Honored Residence","parent_semantic_invariant":"The garden is a protected ecology whose shade, water, growth, and settled enjoyment are produced by sustained care.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garden becomes canopy, vineyard, pasture, rain-cloud, well, bucket, camel trough, catchment, fertile soil, and hospitable station.","key":"B","name":"Rain, well, bucket, and trough","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water descends, collects in rock and well, is drawn by bucket, poured into a trough, and approached by animals.","active_motifs":"rain-holding rock hollow or new well `quranic:root_000434:B011/m01`; layered rain-cloud `quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`; abundant fresh water `quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`; deep abundant well `quranic:root_001040:B005/m01`; great water bucket `quranic:root_001077:B002/m01`; poured well-water and overflowing trough `quranic:root_001077:B003/m01`; watering camels at their mouths `quranic:root_001198:B014/m01`; returning camels for another drink `quranic:root_000464:B007/m01`; water-holding place `quranic:root_001667:B005/m01`; renewing rain `quranic:root_001055:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:19,39` خُلِق/خَلَق (`خ ل ق`); `70:24,39` مَعْلُوم/يَعْلَمُون (`ع ل م`); `70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:32` عَهْد (`ع ه د`); `70:36` قِبَل (`ق ب ل`); `70:38` يُدْخَل (`د خ ل`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:43` يُوفِضُون (`و ف ض`).","synthesis":"The water system is a causal chain from cloud to hollow, well, bucket, trough, and drinking herd. A garden of delight is therefore infrastructural as well as scenic: its ease depends on recurrent delivery and managed access.","ayah_refs":["70:19","70:24","70:27","70:32","70:36","70:38","70:40","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Coveted Ingress, Decoy, and Trap","parent_semantic_invariant":"Desire for an interior can be manipulated by a visible lure, converting self-directed approach into capture.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garden entry becomes a hunting scene with decoy bird, set snare, raised marker, game, and protective screen.","key":"C","name":"Screen and shield cannot create belonging","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A would-be entrant equips himself with cover, shield, or tent, but these defenses only hide and do not authorize entry.","active_motifs":"protective shield `quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`; armor `quranic:root_000121:B005/m01`; mosquito-screen tent `quranic:root_001315:B006/m01`; material covering `quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`; rhetorical concealment `quranic:root_001642:B005/m01`; concealed sword under a garment `quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:44` أَبْصَار (`ب ص ر`); `70:31` وَرَاء (`و ر ي`); `70:35,38` جَنَّة (`ج ن ن`); `70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:38` كُل (`ك ل ل`).","synthesis":"Cover and shield can prevent contact or detection, but they cannot establish a rightful place inside. The concealed weapon even makes defensive appearance compatible with hostile intent, sharpening the difference between protected residence and disguised intrusion.","ayah_refs":["70:44","70:31","70:35","70:36","70:37","70:38"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Radial Crowd and Segmented Entitlement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A crowd arranges itself around a center in directional groups, yet each member converts collective proximity into an individual claim.","parent_surprising_reach":"The crowd becomes a radial diagram of face, neck, side, tribe, faction, mixed host, totality, and burdensome dependent.","key":"A","name":"Projected face, neck, and side","reading_type":"surface-primary","scene_or_process":"Bodies lean and rush toward a center while their right and left sides define the surrounding field.","active_motifs":"direct facing `quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`; direction or source `quranic:root_001198:B003/m01`; body-mark oriented toward a side `quranic:root_001198:B011/m01`; capacity to face a force `quranic:root_001198:B013/m01`; fixed forward gaze `quranic:root_001594:B001/m01`; projected neck and head `quranic:root_001594:B002/m01`; rushing approach `quranic:root_001594:B003/m01`; right side `quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`; left side `quranic:root_000819:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:30,37` أَيْمَان/الْيَمِين (`ي م ن`); `70:36` قِبَلَك مُهْطِعِين (`ق ب ل`, `ه ط ع`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`).","synthesis":"The crowd is first anatomical geometry: face and gaze project inward, neck lengthens, and right/left divide the ring. Capacity to face a force remains merely positional; it does not imply fitness for the center being faced.","ayah_refs":["70:30","70:36","70:37"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Radial Crowd and Segmented Entitlement","parent_semantic_invariant":"A crowd arranges itself around a center in directional groups, yet each member converts collective proximity into an individual claim.","parent_surprising_reach":"The crowd becomes a radial diagram of face, neck, side, tribe, faction, mixed host, totality, and burdensome dependent.","key":"B","name":"Factions around the center","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Previously dispersed persons cluster into bands, tribes, and mixed contingents without becoming one integrated community.","active_motifs":"separated human groups `quranic:root_001011:B002/m01`; encompassing from all sides `quranic:root_000819:B001/m01`; reunion after dispersal `quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`; numerous companies `quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`; herd-like band `quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`; facing tribe or category `quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`; mixed contingents `quranic:root_001667:B002/m01`; gathered crowds `quranic:root_001315:B009/m01`; mass of people `quranic:root_000266:B013/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:27-28,40` رَب (`ر ب ب`); `70:35,38` جَنَّة (`ج ن ن`); `70:36` قِبَل (`ق ب ل`); `70:37` الشِّمَال، عِزِين (`ش م ل`, `ع ز و`); `70:38` كُل (`ك ل ل`); `70:43` يُوفِضُون (`و ف ض`).","synthesis":"Encirclement can look like unity while preserving factional separation. Tribe, band, mixed contingent, and crowd all share location but not covenant; their arrangement around the center does not make them its community.","ayah_refs":["70:27","70:35","70:36","70:37","70:38","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Horizon Oath and Sovereign Replacement","parent_semantic_invariant":"Rule over recurring horizons establishes the capacity to redesign and replace a population before it can escape.","parent_surprising_reach":"Cosmic horizon, legal division, measured design, proxy, changed form, and footrace are one executable proof.","key":"D","name":"The pursued cannot get ahead","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A runner accelerates and tries to create an unbridgeable lead, but pursuit remains able to reach him.","active_motifs":"forward precedence and racing `quranic:root_000671:B001/m01`; escaping beyond a pursuer's reach `quranic:root_000671:B004/m01`; rapid approach `quranic:root_001594:B003/m01`; speed in movement and action `quranic:root_000698:B001/m01`; front rank of a moving host `quranic:root_000698:B002/m01`; urgent rushing `quranic:root_001667:B001/m01`; capacity to execute `quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` مُهْطِعِين (`ه ط ع`); `70:40` قَادِرُون (`ق د ر`); `70:41` مَسْبُوقِين (`س ب ق`); `70:43` سِرَاعًا، يُوفِضُون (`س ر ع`, `و ف ض`).","synthesis":"“Not outstripped” becomes a chase relation. Speed, front rank, urgent rush, and attempted escape measure the creature's motion; sovereign capacity measures the pursuer's continued ability to close it.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:40","70:41","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Wading Play Dissolved into Encounter","parent_semantic_invariant":"Aimless activity immerses its participants in a mixed medium until the promised event interrupts and gathers the scattered motion.","parent_surprising_reach":"Futile discourse becomes water-crossing, stirred drink, saliva, venom, wind-erased ground, discarded object, and joined endpoints.","key":"D","name":"Wind erases the playground","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Wind moves through an exposed place until prior tracks and structures are effaced.","active_motifs":"wind wearing away a dwelling `quranic:root_001358:B005/m01`; broad darkness or water that covers `quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`; north wind and its cooling action `quranic:root_000819:B007/m01`; soft south wind `quranic:root_001525:B009/m01`; western direction and setting `quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:38` نَعِيم (`ن ع م`); `70:40` مَغَارِب (`غ ر ب`); `70:42` يَلْعَبُوا (`ل ع ب`).","synthesis":"Play's “place” is not durable. Opposed winds traverse it, darkness covers it, and setting removes the light by which its traces were visible. The scene gives the command to leave them a temporal weathering process.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:37","70:38","70:40","70:42"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Grave Exit, Target-Rush, and Exhausted Finish","parent_semantic_invariant":"Burial enclosure opens into accelerated collective movement toward a raised objective, ending in bodily lowering and fatigue.","parent_surprising_reach":"Resurrection becomes exhumation, race start, vanguard, quiver, erected target, all-day march, stopped limbs, and missed deadline.","key":"B","name":"Sprint toward the erected object","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A vanguard accelerates toward an upright marker while mixed groups carry the equipment of pursuit.","active_motifs":"rapid motion `quranic:root_000698:B001/m01`; leading runners `quranic:root_000698:B002/m01`; sudden rapid occurrence `quranic:root_000698:B003/m01`; erected marker `quranic:root_001507:B001/m01`; cultic stone `quranic:root_001507:B002/m01`; boundary marker `quranic:root_001507:B003/m01`; urgent rush `quranic:root_001667:B001/m01`; mixed rushing groups `quranic:root_001667:B002/m01`; quiver `quranic:root_001667:B003/m01`; forward rush `quranic:root_001594:B003/m01`; direct facing `quranic:root_001198:B001/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:36` قِبَلَك مُهْطِعِين (`ق ب ل`, `ه ط ع`); `70:43` سِرَاعًا، نُصُب، يُوفِضُون (`س ر ع`, `ن ص ب`, `و ف ض`).","synthesis":"The resurrection crowd behaves like a released field: front runners, mixed contingents, quiver, marker, and urgent acceleration. The target is visually clear, but the comparison destabilizes whether the rush is devotional approach or compelled convergence.","ayah_refs":["70:36","70:43"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Textile Cover, Hidden Interior, and Chromatic Exposure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A surface may shelter or disguise an interior, but wear, opening, and color eventually disclose what the cover carried.","parent_surprising_reach":"Night-cover, wrap, mosquito tent, worn cloth, concealed sword, inner corruption, dyed sign, black raven, and whitening hair form a disclosure process.","key":"A","name":"Wrapped and tented enclosure","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Darkness and cloth wrap an occupant in nested, portable shelter.","active_motifs":"general concealment `quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`; night covering the world `quranic:root_000266:B002/m01`; material cover `quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`; dark engulfing cover `quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`; wrapping in a cloak `quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`; concealed burden under clothing `quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`; encircling crown or canopy `quranic:root_001315:B005/m01`; fine protective tent `quranic:root_001315:B006/m01`; worn cloth stripped of nap `quranic:root_000434:B009/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:19,39` خُلِق/خَلَق (`خ ل ق`); `70:35,38` جَنَّة (`ج ن ن`); `70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:38` كُل (`ك ل ل`).","synthesis":"Covering progresses from night to wrap, cloak, canopy, and tent. The worn-fabric motif introduces duration: every portable shelter thins through use, making eventual exposure a property of the material itself.","ayah_refs":["70:19","70:35","70:36","70:37","70:38"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Textile Cover, Hidden Interior, and Chromatic Exposure","parent_semantic_invariant":"A surface may shelter or disguise an interior, but wear, opening, and color eventually disclose what the cover carried.","parent_surprising_reach":"Night-cover, wrap, mosquito tent, worn cloth, concealed sword, inner corruption, dyed sign, black raven, and whitening hair form a disclosure process.","key":"B","name":"Concealed interior and disguised breach","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A protected inside stores heart, weapon, secret, or corruption, and may present a misleading outer face.","active_motifs":"hidden heart and inward matter `quranic:root_000266:B010/m01`; rhetorical cover that displays other than intended `quranic:root_001642:B005/m01`; private interior `quranic:root_000464:B003/m01`; concealed corruption `quranic:root_000464:B004/m01`; interlocking internal parts `quranic:root_000464:B008/m01`; denial covering truth `quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`; confused intermixture `quranic:root_001423:B009/m01`; concealed sword or calamity `quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:20-21` مَسَّه (`م س س`); `70:31` وَرَاء (`و ر ي`); `70:35,38` جَنَّة (`ج ن ن`); `70:36` كَفَرُوا (`ك ف ر`); `70:37` الشِّمَال (`ش م ل`); `70:38` يُدْخَل (`د خ ل`).","synthesis":"Interior is morally neutral until its contents are known: heart, interlocked structure, weapon, and corruption can all sit behind a cover. Disguise occurs when the outer surface asserts protection while the inner assembly carries breach.","ayah_refs":["70:20","70:31","70:35","70:36","70:37","70:38"]},{"parent_channel":"[P2 70:19-44] Joint, Support, and the Upright Load","parent_semantic_invariant":"A stable order depends on fitted joints and upright supports that distribute weight without opening a dangerous gap.","parent_surprising_reach":"Hip socket, interlocked flesh, shoe straps, rib cage, chest, table leg, well apparatus, straight path, exact weight, crossbeam, and exhausted support form one structural field.","key":"A","name":"Fitted joints around a gap","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Separate components meet at seams and joints, leaving controlled openings while forming a load-bearing body.","active_motifs":"fitting joint or socket `quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`; interlocking tissue and joints `quranic:root_000464:B008/m01`; paired straps and joined parts `quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`; opening between components `quranic:root_001139:B001/m01`; stitched panels `quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`; rib-cage bones `quranic:root_000266:B016/m01`; chest flesh `quranic:root_000095:B003/m01`; chest as structural front `quranic:root_001315:B007/m01`.","ayah_anchors":"`70:24` حَق (`ح ق ق`); `70:29` فُرُوج (`ف ر ج`); `70:35,38` جَنَّة (`ج ن ن`); `70:36` قِبَل (`ق ب ل`); `70:38` يُدْخَل، كُل (`د خ ل`, `ك ل ل`); `70:41` نُبَدِّل (`ب د ل`); `70:44` أَبْصَار (`ب ص ر`).","synthesis":"Stability is not seamlessness. Socket, seam, strap, rib, and chest permit distinct pieces to move together while controlling their gaps. ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
